echo "===== TREE SUMMARY ====="

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib, os, stat, sys

a = Path(sys.argv[1])
b = Path(sys.argv[2])

def sha(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with p.open("rb") as f:
        for c in iter(lambda: f.read(1024*1024), b""):
            h.update(c)
    return h.hexdigest()

def tree(root):
    out = {}
    for p in root.rglob("*"):
        rel = str(p.relative_to(root))
        st = p.lstat()

        if p.is_symlink():
            out[rel] = ("L", os.readlink(p))
        elif p.is_file():
            out[rel] = ("F", st.st_size, sha(p))
        elif p.is_dir():
            out[rel] = ("D",)
    return out

A = tree(a)
B = tree(b)

only17 = sorted(set(A) - set(B))
only20 = sorted(set(B) - set(A))
changed = sorted(p for p in set(A) & set(B) if A[p] != B[p])

print(f"V17_ENTRIES={len(A)}")
print(f"V20_ENTRIES={len(B)}")
print(f"ONLY_V17={len(only17)}")
print(f"ONLY_V20={len(only20)}")
print(f"CHANGED={len(changed)}")

print("CHANGED_FILES=" + ",".join(changed))

for p in changed:
    print(f"{p}")
    print(f"  V17={A[p]}")
    print(f"  V20={B[p]}")
PY

################################################################
# V17 final SELinux type source correction

Earlier source mapping identified the correct source file but reported:

```
V17_LAST_TYPE_SOURCE=device/lineage/sepolicy/common/vendor/property.te:61
```

Direct inspection establishes the actual declaration mechanism as:

```
device/lineage/sepolicy/common/vendor/property.te:11

vendor_public_prop(vendor_fm_radio_app_prop);
```

The generated vendor CIL still confirms that
`vendor_fm_radio_app_prop` is the final V17 type at ordinal 181.

Therefore the previous `:61` value must not be treated as the literal
source-file line number.

V21 is anchored after the actual macro invocation above.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0

FILE=/home/armol/androido_dalykai/LieppOS custom ROM/ulefone-gq5012bf1-research/slot-b-investigation/untouched-b-early-boot-forensics/slot-b-write-preflight/V17-ISOLATION-CONCLUSION.md

################################################################
# V17 policy-off isolation conclusion

V17 was constructed with:

- vendor SELinux artifacts byte-identical to V15
- module-loader/runtime files byte-identical to V16
- frozen V12 system_b unchanged
- root vbmeta_b and vbmeta_system_b unchanged

V16 had left every seeded Android-init marker at zero.

V17 produced fresh:

    v15-mtkgki-early-init=1
    v15-after-ready-zero=1
    v15-after-insmod-start=1

and later progressed through:

    v9-system-pfsd-start
    v10-before-keymaster
    v10-after-keymaster
    v9-keymaster-passed
    v9-before-apex-wait
    v9-apex-activated
    v9-derive-sdk-passed
    v9-init-user0-passed
    v9-restorecon-data-passed
    v9-derive-classpath-passed
    v9-art-boot-passed
    v9-before-odsign-key
    v9-odsign-key-done
    v9-snapshotde-passed
    v8-mtk-pfsd-start

V17 then retained:

    v15-insmod-running=0
    v15-insmod-stopped=0
    v8-modules-ready-passed=0

Therefore the V17 control strongly isolates the V16 SELinux payload, or
filesystem labeling induced by that payload, as the cause of the new
pre-early-init regression.

Separately, V17 reproduces the previously established MTK modules-ready
producer failure when the reconstructed init.insmod.sh domain is absent.

Stock Slot-A provenance also showed vendor_init set/read access to
vendor_mtk_device_prop. The V16 reconstruction did not yet reproduce those
vendor_init permissions.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0

# V17 regeneration OUT-variable correction

The V17 regeneration build completed successfully with BUILD_RC=0.

The initial artifact-copy step failed because Android envsetup/lunch overwrote the
shell variable named OUT, changing it from vendor/etc/selinux to PRODUCT_OUT.

The build itself was not affected.

V21 source was restored after the build and before any subsequent rebuild.

################################################################
# V20 unused dummy-type conclusion

V20 was designed as a minimal control against V17.

V17 did not boot successfully; it is a historical policy/artifact baseline only.

V20 differs from V17's vendor SELinux policy by exactly one unused bare
SELinux type plus its automatically generated object_r roletype:

    type lieppos_v20_dummy_type;

The V17 -> V20 compact audit proved that deleting those two generated CIL
lines makes vendor_sepolicy.cil byte-identical to V17.

V20 runtime result:

- v15-mtkgki-early-init remained 0
- its mtime remained exactly the seed time:
  2026-09-24 00:47:32.952000092 +0300
- no Android-init checkpoint advanced
- V20 vendor_b remained exact
- frozen V12 system_b remained exact
- vbmeta_vendor_b, vbmeta_system_b and root vbmeta_b remained exact

Therefore an unused additional vendor SELinux type is sufficient to
reproduce the pre-early-init regression.

This rules out vendor_mtk_device_prop-specific semantics as the primary
cause of the V19 failure.

The investigation should now move to the consequences of changing the
compiled/precompiled vendor policy type layout itself: type IDs, split
policy/precompiled policy compatibility, or first-stage SELinux loading.

V20_PRE_EARLY_INIT_REPRODUCED=1
CAPTURE_PHONE_MODIFIED=0
EXPERIMENT_PHONE_MODIFIED=1

################################################################
# V20 installation provenance correction

The first V20 install retry succeeded.

Successful install research commit:

    be6c091

It wrote and verified:

VENDOR_B_SHA256=ac3a557e1b0e92ea8b9138a7de1429fd3eb892112e4499b4e9f6b3302712731d
VBMETA_VENDOR_B_FIRST4096_SHA256=93bfd8880742d46385b0cc5ef3d4005bc665852ae827e80256410fad6a60d9c7

and ended with:

    WRITE_DONE=1
    V20_INSTALL_OK=1
    CURRENT=0
    ACTIVE=0
    PHONE_MODIFIED=1

The install block was then accidentally executed a second time.

That second execution saw V20 already installed, while its pre-write gate
still expected V19. It therefore stopped with:

    PREWRITE_STATE_OK=0
    WRITE_DONE=0
    V20_INSTALL_OK=0
    PHONE_MODIFIED=0

Commit e835349 records that second no-op execution and must not be
interpreted as failure of the actual V20 installation.

Fresh live verification after both executions:

VENDOR_B_SHA256=ac3a557e1b0e92ea8b9138a7de1429fd3eb892112e4499b4e9f6b3302712731d

lieppos_make_slotb_vendor() {

    SRC="/mnt/SamsungSSD/android/lineage-22.2"

    RESEARCH="/home/armol/androido_dalykai/LieppOS custom ROM/ulefone-gq5012bf1-research"

    RDIR="$RESEARCH/slot-b-investigation/untouched-b-early-boot-forensics/slot-b-write-preflight"

    PAIRDIR="$RESEARCH/slot-b-investigation/untouched-b-early-boot-forensics/fixed-vendor-vbmeta-regeneration"

    BUILT_VENDOR="$SRC/out/target/product/armol/vendor.img"

    FIT_VENDOR="$SRC/out/target/product/armol/vendor.slotb-fit.img"

    CHILD="$PAIRDIR/vbmeta_vendor.fixed.img"

    VENDOR_KEY="/mnt/SamsungSSD/TIER3/phase4/private/avb-bringup/vendor.pem"

    AVBTOOL="/home/armol/.local/bin/avbtool"

    TARGET_SIZE=816349184
    AVB_MAX_ORIGINAL=803381248
    FIXED_ORIGINAL=800980992

    EXPECTED_BUILT_SHA="850a58b16357597e0fa93f7d5ab9443c85e1b49df33b0e86fa1d2d393044ca50"

    EXPECTED_CHILD_SHA="1ac2a85791813b70f7a901c73d43a19d1358ba0c4158a7e20688f32a2ffe783f"


    echo
    echo "======================================================================"
    echo "  BUILD SLOT-B-SIZED FIXED vendor.img"
    echo "======================================================================"
    echo
    echo "PHONE_OPERATIONS=0"
    echo "FLASH_OPERATIONS=0"
    echo "SUPER_METADATA_CHANGES=0"
    echo "SLOT_CHANGES=0"


    mkdir -p "$RDIR" || {
        echo "STOP: cannot create research directory"
        return 2
    }


    ###########################################################################
    # 1. INPUT IDENTITIES
    ###########################################################################

    echo
    echo "===== INPUT PREFLIGHT ====="


    if [ ! -f "$BUILT_VENDOR" ]; then
        echo "STOP: built vendor missing"
        return 3
    fi


    if [ ! -f "$CHILD" ]; then
        echo "STOP: fixed vbmeta_vendor missing"
        return 3
    fi


    if [ ! -f "$VENDOR_KEY" ]; then
        echo "STOP: vendor signing key missing"
        return 3
    fi


    BUILT_SHA="$(sha256sum "$BUILT_VENDOR" | awk '{print $1}')"

    CHILD_SHA="$(sha256sum "$CHILD" | awk '{print $1}')"


    echo "BUILT_VENDOR_SHA256=$BUILT_SHA"
    echo "FIXED_VBMETA_VENDOR_SHA256=$CHILD_SHA"


    if [ "$BUILT_SHA" != "$EXPECTED_BUILT_SHA" ]; then
        echo "STOP: built vendor hash changed"
        return 4
    fi


    if [ "$CHILD_SHA" != "$EXPECTED_CHILD_SHA" ]; then
        echo "STOP: vbmeta_vendor hash changed"
        return 4
    fi


    HEADROOM=$((AVB_MAX_ORIGINAL - FIXED_ORIGINAL))


    echo
    echo "CURRENT_VENDOR_B_SIZE=$TARGET_SIZE"
    echo "AVB_MAX_ORIGINAL_IMAGE=$AVB_MAX_ORIGINAL"
    echo "FIXED_ORIGINAL_IMAGE=$FIXED_ORIGINAL"
    echo "AVB_ORIGINAL_IMAGE_HEADROOM=$HEADROOM"


    if [ "$FIXED_ORIGINAL" -gt "$AVB_MAX_ORIGINAL" ]; then
        echo "STOP: fixed filesystem exceeds AVB capacity"
        return 5
    fi


    echo "FIXED_ORIGINAL_FITS_CURRENT_VENDOR_B_AVB_CAPACITY=1"


    ###########################################################################
    # 2. SAVE ORIGINAL AVB INFO
    ###########################################################################

    echo
    echo "===== ORIGINAL FIXED VENDOR AVB INFO ====="


    "$AVBTOOL" info_image \
        --image "$BUILT_VENDOR" \
        > "$RDIR/vendor-built-avb-info.txt" 2>&1 ||
    {
        cat "$RDIR/vendor-built-avb-info.txt"
        echo "STOP: cannot inspect original fixed vendor"
        return 6
    }


    sed -n '1,100p' "$RDIR/vendor-built-avb-info.txt"


    ###########################################################################
    # 3. COPY â€ƒ´ NEVER MODIFY BUILD OUTPUT
    ###########################################################################

    echo
    echo "===== CREATE WORKING COPY ====="


    rm -f "$FIT_VENDOR"


    if ! cp --reflink=auto "$BUILT_VENDOR" "$FIT_VENDOR"; then
        echo "STOP: could not create working copy"
        return 7
    fi


    COPY_SHA="$(sha256sum "$FIT_VENDOR" | awk '{print $1}')"


    echo "PRE_RESIZE_COPY_SHA256=$COPY_SHA"


    if [ "$COPY_SHA" != "$BUILT_SHA" ]; then
        echo "STOP: working copy differs before resize"
        return 7
    fi


    ###########################################################################
    # 4. RESIZE AVB ENVELOPE ONLY
    ###########################################################################

    echo
    echo "===== AVB RESIZE IMAGE ====="


    if "$AVBTOOL" resize_image \
        --image "$FIT_VENDOR" \
        --partition_size "$TARGET_SIZE"
    then
        echo "AVB_RESIZE_IMAGE=PASS"
    else
        RC=$?
        echo "AVB_RESIZE_IMAGE=FAIL"
        echo "AVB_RESIZE_RC=$RC"
        return 8
    fi


    FIT_SIZE="$(stat -c '%s' "$FIT_VENDOR")"

    FIT_SHA="$(sha256sum "$FIT_VENDOR" | awk '{print $1}')"


    echo "SLOTB_VENDOR_SIZE=$FIT_SIZE"
    echo "SLOTB_VENDOR_SHA256=$FIT_SHA"


    if [ "$FIT_SIZE" -ne "$TARGET_SIZE" ]; then
        echo "STOP: resized image is not exact vendor_b size"
        return 8
    fi


    echo "SLOTB_VENDOR_EXACT_PARTITION_SIZE=1"


    ###########################################################################
    # 5. INSPECT RESIZED IMAGE
    ###########################################################################

    echo
    echo "===== RESIZED AVB INFO ====="


    "$AVBTOOL" info_image \
        --image "$FIT_VENDOR" \
        > "$RDIR/vendor-slotb-fit-avb-info.txt" 2>&1 ||
    {
        cat "$RDIR/vendor-slotb-fit-avb-info.txt"
        echo "STOP: resized image is not parseable by avbtool"
        return 9
    }


    cat "$RDIR/vendor-slotb-fit-avb-info.txt"


    ###########################################################################
    # 6. COMPARE EMBEDDED VBMETA/DESCRIPTORS EXACTLY
    #
    # Exclude outer AVB footer location / total image size.
    # Everything beginning at "Minimum libavb version" describes the embedded
    # vbmeta and descriptors and should remain identical.
    ###########################################################################

    sed -n \
        '/^Minimum libavb version:/,$p' \
        "$RDIR/vendor-built-avb-info.txt" \
        > "$RDIR/vendor-built-semantic-avb.txt"


    sed -n \
        '/^Minimum libavb version:/,$p' \
        "$RDIR/vendor-slotb-fit-avb-info.txt" \
        > "$RDIR/vendor-slotb-fit-semantic-avb.txt"


    if diff -u \
        "$RDIR/vendor-built-semantic-avb.txt" \
        "$RDIR/vendor-slotb-fit-semantic-avb.txt" \
        > "$RDIR/vendor-resize-semantic.diff"
    then
        SEMANTIC_IDENTICAL=1
    else
        SEMANTIC_IDENTICAL=0
    fi


    echo
    echo "EMBEDDED_AVB_SEMANTICS_IDENTICAL=$SEMANTIC_IDENTICAL"


    if [ "$SEMANTIC_IDENTICAL" != "1" ]; then
        echo
        echo "Unexpected AVB semantic difference:"
        cat "$RDIR/vendor-resize-semantic.diff"

################################################################

BYTES=874
################################################################
# V20 type-layout follow-up

V20 decisively reproduced the pre-early-init failure.

The V20 control added only one unused bare vendor SELinux type relative
to the historical V17 vendor policy artifact.

The next question is whether the failure comes from:

1. merely increasing the vendor policy type count, or
2. shifting the ordinal / binary type IDs of existing policy types.

This audit compares the order of every `(type NAME)` declaration in the
V17 and V20 generated vendor CIL.

If many existing types after the dummy move by +1, the next control should
place an unused dummy type at the absolute tail of the vendor policy so
existing type order remains unchanged.

If existing common types do not shift, the investigation should instead
focus directly on total type-count / precompiled binary policy compatibility
and first-stage SELinux loading.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0

################################################################
FILE=/home/armol/androido_dalykai/LieppOS custom ROM/ulefone-gq5012bf1-research/slot-b-investigation/untouched-b-early-boot-forensics/slot-b-write-preflight/V20-TYPE-ORDER-CLARIFICATION.md
BYTES=1309
################################################################
# V20 type-order clarification

V20 decisively reproduced the pre-early-init failure, but its generated
vendor policy changed in two separable ways relative to the historical V17 policy artifact:

- vendor type count increased from 181 to 182;
- the V20 dummy type was inserted at ordinal 155;
- 27 existing types moved by +1;
- the shifted range begins with proc_deny_new_usb at 155 -> 156;
- the shifted range ends with vendor_fm_radio_app_prop at 181 -> 182.

Therefore V20 proves that adding the otherwise-unused type at that position
is sufficient to reproduce the failure, but it does not distinguish between:

1. failure caused by increasing/changing the total compiled vendor policy;
2. failure caused by changing the numeric/ordinal IDs of existing types.

The next useful control is V21: add one unused vendor type only after all
181 V17 types, producing dummy ordinal 182 while preserving every existing
V17 type ordinal exactly.

V21 must be audited before flashing. Required generated-policy properties:

    V21_TYPE_COUNT=182
    DUMMY_TYPE_ORDINAL=182
    SHIFTED_COMMON_TYPE_COUNT=0
    ONLY_V21_TYPE=one dummy type

The exact policy-directory ordering should be established before constructing
V21 rather than assuming that lexical .te filenames determine final CIL order.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0

################################################################

################################################################
# V21 attempt 1: Android envsetup failure

The first V21 construction attempt successfully reached the intended
experimental source layout:

- V20 dummy declaration disabled;
- V21 dummy inserted after the final V17 source declaration mechanism;
- phone untouched.

The build did not actually start.

The shell running the experiment had `set -u` / `nounset` enabled.
Android's `build/envsetup.sh` referenced `TOP` before it had been initialized,
causing:

    build/envsetup.sh: line 21: TOP: unbound variable
    Couldn't locate the top of the tree.

This is a host-shell/script error, not evidence about V21 SELinux policy.

The experiment automatically restored both modified source files afterward.

The retry therefore runs the Android build environment without shell
`nounset`.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0

################################################################
# V21 pre-flash verdict

## Question

Does adding one otherwise-unused vendor SELinux type preserve every existing
V17 type ordinal when the new type is appended after the actual final V17
type source declaration?

## V17

- type count: 181
- known-good control
- final type: `vendor_fm_radio_app_prop`
- ordinal: 181
- corrected source:
  `device/lineage/sepolicy/common/vendor/property.te:11`
- source mechanism:
  `vendor_public_prop(vendor_fm_radio_app_prop);`

## V20

- type count: 182
- dummy ordinal: 155
- 27 existing types shifted
- reproduced the pre-early-init failure

## V21

```
ENVSETUP_RC=0
LUNCH_CHOICE=lineage_armol-bp1a-user
LUNCH_RC=0
BUILD_TARGET=vendor_sepolicy.cil
BUILD_RC=0

V17_TYPE_COUNT=181
V21_TYPE_COUNT=182

ONLY_V17_TYPE_COUNT=0
ONLY_V21_TYPE_COUNT=1
ONLY_V21_TYPE=lieppos_v21_dummy_type

V20_DUMMY_PRESENT=0
DUMMY_TYPE_ORDINAL=182

SHIFTED_COMMON_TYPE_COUNT=0
ORDINALS_1_TO_181_IDENTICAL=1

V21_CIL_REFERENCE_COUNT=2
V21_UNEXPECTED_REFERENCE_COUNT=0

AUDIT_RC=0
SEMANTIC_AUDIT_RC=0

V21_FLASH_GATE=PASS
SOURCE_LEFT_AS_V21=1
PHONE_MODIFIED=0
```

## Interpretation after eventual Slot-B test

If V21 boots while V20 fails, the isolated evidence points toward the V20
renumbering of existing vendor SELinux types being required for that failure.

If V21 reproduces the same pre-early-init failure while V17 ordinals 1..181
remain identical, existing-type renumbering is not required. Investigation
should move toward type-table growth, policy serialization/layout, or another
correlated structural policy property.

If V21 behaves differently from both controls, preserve the state and compare
the generated and binary policy artifacts before drawing a conclusion.

FILE=/home/armol/androido_dalykai/LieppOS custom ROM/ulefone-gq5012bf1-research/slot-b-investigation/untouched-b-early-boot-forensics/slot-b-write-preflight/V21-VARIANT-CONFOUNDER-CORRECTION.md
BYTES=609
################################################################
# V21 variant confounder correction

The first V21 Slot-B boot test used a `user` build.

Historical V17 and V20 experiments used `userdebug`.

A userdebug regeneration with the V21 dummy removed reproduced the historical
V17 `vendor_sepolicy.cil` and `precompiled_sepolicy` hashes exactly.

Therefore the first V21 boot result is confounded by build variant and must not
be used to conclude that existing SELinux type renumbering is unnecessary.

The clean V21 experiment is the userdebug build where removing only the V21
dummy type and roletype returns exactly to the historical V17 CIL.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0

################################################################
FILE=/home/armol/androido_dalykai/LieppOS custom ROM/ulefone-gq5012bf1-research/slot-b-investigation/untouched-b-early-boot-forensics/slot-b-write-preflight/V21-VENDOR-VERIFY-KEY-CORRECTION.md
BYTES=438
################################################################
# V21 vendor verification correction

The first V21 packaging run incorrectly supplied vendor.pem to
`avbtool verify_image` for the resized vendor image.

The proven V20 procedure verifies vendor.img using its embedded AVB key.
vendor.pem is used to sign and verify the generated vbmeta_vendor image.

The earlier key-mismatch was therefore a host verification error, not
evidence that the V21 vendor image was invalid.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0

################################################################
FILE=/home/armol/androido_dalykai/LieppOS custom ROM/ulefone-gq5012bf1-research/slot-b-investigation/untouched-b-early-boot-forensics/V17-BOOT-STATUS-CORRECTION.md
BYTES=737
################################################################
# V17 boot-status correction

V17 was previously described in research notes as a known-good boot baseline.

That is incorrect.

The device owner confirms that V17 never successfully booted.

Therefore:

- V17 must NOT be treated as a known-good runtime baseline.
- V17 is only a historical SELinux/vendor artifact baseline.
- Reproducing V17 CIL and precompiled_sepolicy hashes proves artifact reproducibility,
  not bootability.
- Any prior inference based on "V17 boots while V20/V21 fail" is invalid.
- V20 and V21 are both observed to hang on the boot logo without reaching ADB.
- The current investigation must determine what configuration actually last booted,
  rather than assuming V17 was that configuration.

PHONE_MODIFIED=0
